\texttt{Trial \#3: New plan: I failed because I repeatedly attempted to take the cauldron from the kitchen floor, ignoring the explicit error message that it was "not takeable," and I wasted turns cycling through rooms to find ingredients I already had or couldn't locate. Take all necessary ingredients from the ice box (sandwich, toad, orange, apple, banana, eye of newt) one by one, dropping only the item currently in inventory to make space, rather than trying to take multiple items at once. 3. Return to the kitchen, take the cauldron (which is now accessible after gathering ingredients), and proceed to brew the potion using the collected items, ensuring I do not attempt to take the cauldron again if it is already taken or if the recipe requires specific items first.} \\
